#AOCI-CODE-VOLUME: 1

===/<repository>/===
.editorconfig[FC4T]: F:Root EditorConfig fixing UTF-8, LF line endings, a final newline, two-space indentation and trailing-whitespace trimming for every file. | R:code:.prettierrc | A:Declares itself the top-most file with root = true, so no nested EditorConfig can override it. | S:-
.gitattributes[FC4T]: F:Marks every file as text with LF checkout so all platforms check out byte-identical content; binaries stay auto-detected. | R:code:.editorconfig | A:The header comment explains that a CRLF rewrite on Windows would mark the whole AOCI baseline tree stale. | S:Every file is forced to eol=lf so AOCI's byte-exact baselines stay valid; overriding it marks the tree stale.
.gitignore[FC3T]: F:Git ignore list for editor folders, dependencies, built bundles, maps, vault data, environment files, ACP logs, model cache, and local agent configuration. | R:code:apps/obsidian/esbuild.config.mjs | A:Ignores .vscode/.idea; node_modules; main.js/styles.css; *.map and data.json/data/; .env*; ACP logs, model cache and machine-bound agent files, including .codegraph/ and backup copies of the agent settings | S:The AOCI binding .mcp.json is never committed.
.prettierrc[FC4T]: F:Prettier configuration for LF endings, a 100-column width, two-space tabs, semicolons, double quotes and ES5 trailing commas. | R:code:.editorconfig | A:Overlaps .editorconfig on line endings and indentation; the repo requires npm run format before every pull request. | S:-
AGENTS.md[SG7M]: F:Master agent guide for the repository: monorepo layout, commands, engineering principles, task-specific guides, the Obsidian review gate and the AOCI contract. | R:code:package.json,code:packages/core/package.json,code:packages/protocol/package.json,code:packages/client/package.json,code:apps/obsidian/esbuild.config.mjs | A:Binding rules include never running npm run dev, never editing RELEASES.md outside a release PR, linking an issue in every code comment, and running npm run review:obsidian before a plugin PR; the agent skills section points at the issue tracker, triage labels, GLOSSARY.md and the ADRs. | S:npm run lint rejects any Node, Electron or Obsidian access inside packages/core, packages/protocol and packages/client, and this guide outranks historical memory for AOCI state.
CONTRIBUTING.md[SU4S]: F:Contributor guide for issue reporting, pull request expectations, dev setup, the Obsidian review preflight, commit signing and a manual test checklist. | R:code:package.json, code:.editorconfig, code:.prettierrc, code:.github/workflows/node.js.yml | A:npm test spawns one Jest worker per core minus one, so COPILOT_JEST_MAX_WORKERS caps memory, and npm run test:vault is macOS-only. | S:test:vault symlinks artifacts into the vault plugin folder, not the worktree root, so vault data.json stays local.
LICENSE[SS5L]: F:Verbatim GNU Affero General Public License v3 text that covers the plugin's distribution terms. | R:code:package.json | A:The AGPL-3.0 terms, including the network-use clause, apply to this repository and its built plugin. | S:Verbatim AGPL-3.0 text matching the license field in package.json; the file must ship with the plugin.
README.md[SU5S]: F:Project front page describing the agent-based Copilot experience, setup paths, hosted and BYOK models, plans, FAQ and the paid-plan privacy disclosure. | R:code:package.json, code:.github/ISSUE_TEMPLATE/bug_report.md, code:.github/ISSUE_TEMPLATE/feature_request.md | A:Links the docs for agent mode, projects, skills, commands and providers, and points bug reporters at the in-app Report an issue flow. | S:The disclosure states hosted requests carry a random UUID, while diagnostic reports are deliberately retained for 60 days.
RELEASES.md[SV6L]: F:Release notes for every shipped Copilot version, with emoji-headed sections for enhancements, bug fixes, compatibility notes and troubleshooting. | R:code:.claude/agents/release.md, code:.claude/agents/prerelease.md, code:.github/workflows/release.yml | A:New entries are prepended right after the Release Notes header, newest first, and existing entries are never edited. | S:Only the release and prerelease agents write this file; AGENTS.md forbids editing it outside a release PR.

jest.config.js[XQ5T]: F:Jest configuration that runs the plugin, package, gallery and script suites with ts-jest, jsdom and the plugin's path aliases. | R:code:jest.setup.js,code:apps/obsidian/__mocks__/obsidian.js,code:apps/obsidian/tsconfig.json | A:- | S:moduleNameMapper resolves @copilot/<pkg> to packages/<pkg>/src and maps obsidian, yaml, pkce-challenge and the agent SDKs to local mocks.

version-bump.mjs[OV6T]: F:Release script that stamps the npm package version into the plugin manifest and records the manifest's minAppVersion in versions.json. | R:code:manifest.json,code:versions.json,code:package.json | A:- | S:A prerelease version writes manifest-beta.json and keeps the release manifest as its source, and a release removes manifest-beta.json.
versions.json[FC6S]: F:Map from every released Copilot version to the minimum Obsidian app version that supports it. | R:code:manifest.json,code:version-bump.mjs,code:package.json | A:- | S:The newest entry must match the manifest version and carry the manifest's minAppVersion, since Obsidian reads this map to allow an update.
architecture.debt.json[FQ7L]: F:Allow-list of accepted architecture boundary violations, listing forbidden imports per file and the client files allowed to reach Node, Electron or Obsidian | R:code:architecture.json,code:scripts/architecture.js,code:eslint.architecture.mjs | A:counts, imports, clientAccess | S:Only the totals are compared against origin/master, so moving a file renames its entries; the counts must never grow, so fix an import instead of listing it.
architecture.json[FC7M]: F:Declares the monorepo package boundaries as boxes of file globs and the edges that permit imports between them | R:code:scripts/architecture.js, code:eslint.architecture.mjs, code:architecture.debt.json | A:boxes, edges, runsAnywhere | S:Boxes named in runsAnywhere (core, protocol, client/*) may not reach Node, Electron or Obsidian; host/features/X may also import client/features/X, and every other edge is one-directional.

copilotPlusUsage.ts[IA5T]: F:Turns the Copilot Plus usage snapshot into plan usage windows and reads a model's context limit from the cached catalog | R:code:apps/obsidian/src/LLMProviders/brevilabsClient.ts | A:the windows are five_hour and weekly; an uncapped or unreadable window is left out, never zeroed | S:An absent usage window is indistinguishable from an uncapped one, so a missing window never clears the meters

===/<repository>/apps/obsidian/src/agentMode/acp/===
AcpBackendProcess.ts[IN9L]: F:Runs an ACP agent subprocess as a BackendProcess: negotiates capabilities, translates wire events into session-domain events, and handles permissions. | R:code:apps/obsidian/src/agentMode/acp/wireTranslate.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/agentMode/acp/AcpProcessManager.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/agentMode/acp/VaultClient.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/agentMode/acp/sessionUpdateWireOrder.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/agentMode/session/types.ts | A:Implements the BackendProcess contract: newSession, prompt, cancel and loadSession are the main entry points; every one is guarded by the capability negotiated at initialize. | S:Capabilities come from the initialize reply, and a later method-not-found disables one permanently. MCP servers are sent only to agents that advertised mcp/http; an agent that never advertised it may reject the whole session. Updates that arrive before a handler is registered are buffered (32 max) and replayed later. The agent system prompt rides on session/new, load and resume, so a backend without the systemPrompt capability fails instead of running the chat as Copilot. An internal JSON-RPC error becomes a dead-service report only after a failed session/list probe.
AcpProcessManager.ts[OR6S]: F:Spawns the ACP agent subprocess, exposes its stdio as web streams, logs stderr and stops it with SIGTERM then SIGKILL. | R:code:apps/obsidian/src/agentMode/acp/AcpBackendProcess.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/agentMode/acp/debugTap.ts | A:start() returns { stdin, stdout } web streams and throws when already started; onExit subscribes to process exit; shutdown() escalates SIGTERM to SIGKILL after 3 seconds. | S:stdout lines are stripped of terminal escape sequences before they reach the JSON-RPC reader, because an escape-prefixed line makes the SDK drop the frame and strand initialization.
VaultClient.ts[IS6T]: F:Implements the ACP client side over the Obsidian vault: reads and writes files for the agent and forwards session updates and permission requests. | R:code:apps/obsidian/src/agentMode/acp/AcpBackendProcess.ts | A:readTextFile and writeTextFile resolve the requested path inside the vault and answer the agent; requestPermission delegates to the host's prompter. | S:Paths are confined to the vault: a path resolving outside it, or inside a dot-prefixed first segment, is rejected with an invalid-params error, and only a desktop FileSystemAdapter is supported.
debugTap.ts[OO5S]: F:Taps the ACP subprocess streams to log each JSON-RPC frame and, when enabled, append it to the shared agent activity log. | R:code:apps/obsidian/src/agentMode/acp/AcpBackendProcess.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/agentMode/acp/AcpProcessManager.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/agentMode/session/debugSink.ts | A:wrapStreamsForDebug(stdin, stdout, tag) returns tapped web streams; the NdjsonLineSplitter turns byte chunks into complete lines for both the tap and the stdout sanitizer. | S:Frame records are only written when the agent activity log setting is on; the log keeps the direction (sent or received), the backend tag and the pending request id so a reply can name its method.

sessionUpdateWireOrder.ts[MM6T]: F:Intercepts session/update notifications on the ACP stream and delivers them in wire order, bypassing the SDK's out-of-order dispatch. | R:code:apps/obsidian/src/agentMode/acp/AcpBackendProcess.ts | A:deliverSessionUpdatesInWireOrder(stream, deliver) returns a stream whose session/update notifications are handed to deliver and removed from the SDK path. | S:The SDK settles a response before dispatching notifications read ahead of it, so session/load used to resolve before its replay arrived; routing updates here restores wire order.
types.ts[FG5T]: F:Declares the ACP backend contract: the spawn descriptor, the backend interface and the chat prompt shape a backend may extend. | R:code:apps/obsidian/src/agentMode/acp/AcpBackendProcess.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/agentMode/session/types.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/agentMode/backends/shared/simpleBinaryBackend.ts | A:AcpBackend exposes buildSpawnDescriptor plus optional plan-usage, context-window, turn-diff, copilot-tool, prompt-extension and agent-mode hooks, all of them optional except the descriptor. | S:-

FilterRetriever.ts[DA7M]: F:Standalone retriever that guarantees title, tag and time-range matches as full-note documents ahead of relevance-ranked search | R:code:apps/obsidian/src/search/searchUtils.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/mergeResults.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/SearchCore.ts | A:new FilterRetriever(app, options); getRelevantDocuments(query) -> Document[]; hasTimeRange() | S:Guaranteed matches carry full note text with score 1.0 and source title-match or tag-match, so they survive top-K slicing; tag matching is hierarchical, and a time range caps the window at 365 days.
QueryExpander.ts[AA6M]: F:Expands a search query into alternative phrasings and salient terms via the chat model, with cache and fallback | R:code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/utils/tagUtils.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/utils.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/constants.ts | A:new QueryExpander({ maxVariants, timeout, cacheSize, getChatModel }); expand(query) -> ExpandedQuery | S:The prompt asks for <salient> and <queries> sections only; the XML is parsed from the model output and a missing section falls back to term extraction from the original query with no variants.
README.md[SA6M]: F:Design document for the Search v3 chunk-based lexical retrieval core and its filter/search merge contract | R:code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/SearchCore.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/FilterRetriever.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/mergeResults.ts | A:Documents the v3 pipeline topology; query expansion; grep recall; BM25 ranking and boost formulas; the deprecated status of the tree | S:The document is deprecated: v3 awaits removal and its dedicated tests are retired; part of it is stale against the code, since grep only requires terms of length 2.
SearchCore.ts[AA8M]: F:Orchestrates the lexical retrieval pipeline: query expansion, grep recall, BM25 ranking, folder and graph boosts, normalization and diverse top-K | R:code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/QueryExpander.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/engines/FullTextEngine.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/scanners/GrepScanner.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/scoring/AdaptiveCutoff.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/chunks.ts | A:SearchCore(app, getChatModel?); retrieve(query, options) -> result object with results and queryExpansion | S:Queries longer than 1000 characters are truncated; maxResults is clamped to 1..100 and candidateLimit to 10..1000, and returnAll forces both to 100. Recall terms are the lowercased union of expanded queries and salient terms, while the ranking query is the salient terms and falls back to the original query. Any failure degrades to grep-only ranking scored 1/(index+1), and the MiniSearch index is cleared after each search.
TieredLexicalRetriever.ts[IA6M]: F:LangChain retriever that runs the lexical search core and converts ranked chunk or note ids into Documents | R:code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/SearchCore.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/chunks.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/search/RetrieverFactory.ts | A:new TieredLexicalRetriever(app, options); getRelevantDocuments(query, config?); getLastQueryExpansion() | S:A result id containing # is treated as a chunk id and its text is fetched from the chunk manager; a document is marked includeInContext only when its score exceeds minSimilarityScore (default 0.1).
chunks.ts[KA7L]: F:Splits vault notes into markdown-aware chunks with a note-title header, caching them under a memory budget | R:code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/utils/MemoryManager.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/constants.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/engines/FullTextEngine.ts | A:getSharedChunkManager(app) -> ChunkManager; getChunks(notePaths, opts); getChunkText(id); getChunkTextSync(id) | S:Chunk ids are <note path>#<index>; a cached note is regenerated when the file mtime is newer, and every chunk is prefixed with a NOTE TITLE header so the note name is searchable in each chunk.
interfaces.ts[DA5T]: F:Declares the search option, ranked-result and score-explanation contracts shared by the v3 pipeline | R:code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/SearchCore.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/engines/FullTextEngine.ts | A:SearchOptions; NoteIdRank; SearchExplanation | S:The result id is the chunk id (note_path#chunk_index), and the explanation carries baseScore and finalScore plus optional lexical, folder and graph boost records.
mergeResults.ts[KA5T]: F:Merges filter and search documents, dropping search hits whose note path is already covered by a filter hit | R:code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/FilterRetriever.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/TieredLexicalRetriever.ts | A:mergeFilterAndSearchResults(filterDocs, searchDocs) -> result object with filterResults and searchResults | S:Dedup is one-directional: search documents are removed when their path is in the filter set, and filter documents are never dropped.

===/<repository>/apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/engines/===
FullTextEngine.ts[KA8L]: F:Builds an ephemeral MiniSearch index over candidate note chunks and ranks them with BM25 by weighted fields | R:code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/chunks.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/utils/MemoryManager.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/interfaces.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/constants.ts | A:new FullTextEngine(app, chunkManager?); buildFromCandidates(candidatePaths) -> indexed chunk count; search(queries, limit, salientTerms, originalQuery) | S:Field weights are title 5, tags 4, heading 2.5, path 1.5 and body 1; search uses prefix true, fuzzy false and combineWith OR. The ranking query is the salient terms joined by spaces and falls back to the original query, so expanded variants only widen recall. The tokenizer emits hashed and unhashed tag forms, every hierarchical tag prefix, ASCII words and CJK bigrams. Frontmatter primitive values are appended to the chunk body, and indexing stops when the memory limit is reached.

===/<repository>/apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/scanners/===
GrepScanner.ts[KA6S]: F:Recalls candidate notes by substring-matching recall terms against file paths first and file contents second | R:code:apps/obsidian/src/search/searchUtils.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/SearchCore.ts | A:new GrepScanner(app); batchCachedReadGrep(queries, limit) -> note paths; grep(query, limit) | S:Only terms longer than one character are searched, path matches are ranked first by how many terms they contain, and the content pass fills the remaining slots; excluded notes never enter the scan.

===/<repository>/apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/scoring/===
AdaptiveCutoff.ts[KA6S]: F:Cuts a ranked list down to a score threshold and a result ceiling, preferring one chunk per note | R:code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/utils/chunkIdUtils.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/interfaces.ts | A:adaptiveCutoff(results, config) -> result object with results cutoffScore uniqueNotes and totalBefore; AdaptiveCutoffConfig | S:The threshold is max(topScore * relativeThreshold, absoluteMinScore); the floor keeps a minimum count even below it, and diverse mode adds a second chunk of a note only after every note has one.
FolderBoostCalculator.ts[KA6S]: F:Boosts notes that cluster in a folder, scaling the factor by the folder's share of relevant hits | R:code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/utils/chunkIdUtils.ts,code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/interfaces.ts | A:new FolderBoostCalculator(app?); applyBoosts(results) -> NoteIdRank[] | S:A folder is boosted only when at least 2 of its notes hit and they make up at least 40% of the folder's markdown files; the factor is 1 + log2(count+1) * sqrt(relevanceRatio), capped at 1.15.
GraphBoostCalculator.ts[KA5M]: F:Re-ranks v3 search results by scaling scores with a boost from backlinks, co-citations and shared tags | R:code:apps/obsidian/src/search/v3/utils/chunkIdUtils.ts | A:applyBoost;GraphBoostConfig | S:Candidates collapse to the best chunk per note and are capped at maxCandidates (default 10); a boosted row that had no explanation gets explanation set to undefined
